# Corrections from publications

A few COD CIFs cannot give a correct pattern as deposited, but the original article states what the file leaves out or gets wrong. For these, hsrdb-tools applies a correction after reading the CIF. The CIF files are not changed. The corrections are in [`hsrdb_tools/corrections.json`](../hsrdb_tools/corrections.json), each with its source; the corrected entry says so in its comment in HighScore ("Corrected from the publication: …"), and the build report notes it.

A correction is added only when the article itself gives the missing information, checked by reading it.

| COD | Compound | Problem in the CIF | Correction | Source | Check |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| [1010854](https://www.crystallography.net/cod/1010854.cif) | NiCl₂·6NH₃ | no atoms | Fm-3; the 1922 structure, isomorphous with (NH₄)₂PtCl₆: Ni at 0 0 0, Cl at ¼ ¼ ¼, N at u 0 0 with u = 0.24; H not placed | [Wyckoff, J. Am. Chem. Soc. 44, 1239 (1922)](https://doi.org/10.1021/ja01427a007), p. 1241 | unit-cell content 4 × formula; u is bounded only to 0.227–0.245, so Ni–N 2.42 Å is approximate |

O1 U₃₃ = 0.684 | each interlayer site 0.25 cation (Mg₁.₉₈Al₀.₆₉Cr₀.₂₃Fe₀.₀₈Ni₀.₀₂ per 3) + 0.25 O; O1 U₃₃ = 0.084; the intergrowth of shifted layers as refined (p. 1417): four tetrahedral sites of 15% occupancy and occupancy × 1.15 for octahedral cations, apical O and OH | [Guggenheim and Zhan, Am. The intergrowth atoms and occupancy factors are part of the refined model and shape the pattern, but they describe shifted layers on top of the full layer rather than extra atoms, so they are left out of the formula, density and I/Ic (`scattering_only` in the corrections file). No displacement parameters were published for the intergrowth atoms; they get B = 0.5 Å².

## Skipped CIFs checked against their articles

Skipped CIFs are checked against their original articles one at a time. When the article gives what the CIF lacks, a correction is added above; otherwise the CIF stays skipped and is listed here with the reason.

| COD | Compound | Article | Why it stays skipped |
|---|---|---|---|
| [1001012](https://www.crystallography.net/cod/1001012.cif) | Ca(NO₃)₂·2H₂O | Leclaire and Monier, [Acta Cryst. A33, 717 (1977)](https://doi.org/10.1107/S0567739477001843) | the article studies crystal habits and a phase transformation and contains no crystal structure; the CIF has no cell, symmetry or atoms. The two structures it discusses are in COD as [1001023](https://www.crystallography.net/cod/1001023.cif) (β, C2/c) and [1001000](https://www.crystallography.net/cod/1001000.cif) (α, Ccca) |
